5.10 What the validation establishes1136
Taken together, these tests show practical mean closure and stable signal recovery for the source-1137
like spectra that were studied. The training exclusion keeps the GP from learning the signal tails,1138
the pseudo-data means are ﬁtted independently of the GP, and every pseudo-spectrum receives a1139
fresh sideband ﬁt. Numerical branches are chosen without reference to the ﬁtted signal or pull.1140
Near threshold, the historical analytic family is replaced only in the two cells where its description1141
of the source is inadequate; tables and ﬁgures identify those replacements explicitly.1142

Under the adopted practical criterion, the near-threshold concern is resolved within this source-1143
conditioned suite. The two replacement 100-background intervals and their independent reserve1144
samples lie inside the ±0. 5 mean-pull band. So do all twenty zero-signal point estimates and all1145
eighty correlated injected-strength point estimates in the four-lane display. The paired response1146
remains 96.6–96.9%. The practical mean-bias requirement is therefore met for these tests, provided1147
that each replacement remains identiﬁed by its generating model and GP support.1148
The stricter diagnostics remain important. The original 10% × 10, 180 MeV cell retains an1149
68

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
exploratory Holm-20 ﬂag with mean 0.338, and the native-10%, 65 MeV replacement retains a1150
targeted Holm-2 ﬂag. Three of twenty zero-signal width intervals and ﬁfteen of eighty all-strength1151
width intervals exclude one; the stable signal attenuation is 3.1–3.4%; and the ﬁxed-hyperparameter1152
tail-removal shifts are nonzero. The study therefore does not prove zero bias for arbitrary smooth1153
spectra, exact pull calibration, conﬁdence-limit coverage, bias in the observed data, or a scan-1154
global signiﬁcance calibration. Any larger study should retain the source, support, optimizer, and1155
post-result tolerance labels used here.1156
5.11 Selecting the 2021 GP support1157
The signal search tests masses from 50 to 250 MeV, but the GP also needs data outside that interval1158
to constrain the background near its endpoints. At low mass, the native 2021 spectrum has a sparse1159
shoulder followed by a rapid turn-on: its 0.125 MeV bins contain only a few hundred events around1160
30–32 MeV and several thousand by 40 MeV. We therefore compared several lower support edges1161
while holding the upper edge at 300 MeV and leaving the search interval unchanged. The aim was1162
to exclude the least stable part of the turn-on without discarding useful sideband information.1163
5.11.1 Source shape and candidate supports1164
Figure 67 shows the source spectrum and the tested lower edges. The 30 MeV setting is retained1165
as a control but was not eligible for selection; the candidate edges were 32, 34, 36, 38, and 40 MeV.1166

5.11.2 Initial scan1167
The ﬁrst stage reuses the 100 source-conditioned backgrounds archived with v4.9.1; no new pseudo-1168
data are generated. At each support edge, matched-reference extractions are performed at 55, 60,1169
65, and 70 MeV with 0-, 2-, and 5- σA injections. The scan changes only the lower support edge. Its1170
binning, preprocessing, blind and training masks, kernel bounds, signed extraction, and optimizer1171
checks are inherited from the v4.2/v4.5 analysis deﬁnition. Optimizer branches are chosen using ﬁt1172
reproducibility, covariance validity, kernel state, and GP log marginal likelihood; neither the pull1173
nor any limit or p-value enters that choice.1174
The ﬁrst 25 backgrounds were processed at all six edges. All 1800 ﬁts passed the numerical1175
checks, with no invalid covariance matrix or kernel-bound contact. Under the original rule, an edge1176
was rejected when any injection cell had |p| ≥0. 5 and its two-sided 90% Student- t interval excluded1177
zero. Every candidate was rejected. Table 11 records that outcome under the rule used for the1178
initial scan.1179
Table 11: Initial 25-background minimax summary. Every candidate is rejected by the original
injection criterion. The 30 MeV row is a control and was never eligible for selection.

5.11.3 Practical criterion and independent conﬁrmation1180
After the ﬁrst stage, we adopted a less stringent practical criterion: at least 9 of the 12 mass–1181
injection means and at least 3 of the 4 zero-signal means had to satisfy |p|< 0. 75, and no mean1182
could reach 1.25 in magnitude. The numerical checks, the minimax score, and the 0.10 tie margin1183
were left unchanged. Among candidates within that margin, the lower edge was preferred so that1184
the ﬁt retained more data. Because this criterion was formulated after the ﬁrst 25 backgrounds had1185
been examined, it is a post-selection practical rule, not a predeclared equivalence test or a coverage1186
criterion. It was ﬁxed before the remaining 75 backgrounds were processed.1187
Only the 36 and 38 MeV candidates pass the practical rule in the ﬁrst stage. The 38 MeV edge1188
has the smaller worst-cell pull, but 36 MeV lies within the retained tie margin and is therefore1189
selected for conﬁrmation. The independent continuation uses backgrounds 25–99 at 34, 36, and1190
38 MeV. At 36 MeV, 10 of 12 cell means and all four zero-signal means satisfy the 0.75 condition1191
in the continuation alone; the worst magnitude is 0.901. In the complete 100-background sample,1192
the corresponding counts are 9 of 12 and 3 of 4, with a worst magnitude of 0.960. Every ﬁt passes1193
the numerical checks. With the criterion already ﬁxed, these results freeze the 2021 GP support at1194
36–300 MeV without further tuning.1195
At the selected edge, the full-sample means at 55 MeV are −0. 773, −0. 841, and −0. 960 for the1196
0-, 2-, and 5- σA injections. Their 90% intervals exclude zero, so the low-mass under-recovery is1197
retained as a real limitation of this test. Across the nine cells from 60 to 70 MeV, the means range1198

Each accepted toy also provides a bounded Wald ˜qµ 90% CL s yield limit. We use those values1201

5.12 Full-2016 GP-support qualiﬁcation1206
Version 4.9.7 repeats the lower-support study for full-2016 inference before constructing any support-1207
speciﬁc observed result. The search interval remains 39–180 MeV and the upper GP-support edge1208
remains 210 MeV. Candidate lower edges are 28, 29, 30, 31, 32, and 33 MeV; 34 MeV is an ineligible1209
geometry control because only four rebinned low-side training-bin centers remain at the 39 MeV1210
search endpoint. The native 0.05 MeV bins are rebinned by ﬁve, so the integer-MeV edge steps1211
retain a common 0.25 MeV coarse-bin phase.1212
5.12.1 Conditional generating construction1213
The low-mass shape is derived from the pre-existing 2016 10% development sample/subset. Its1214
statistical disjointness from the full sample is not established, so it supplies partial observed-shape1215
information and is not called independent here. Before the support decision, the full-2016 value1216
array enters only through one scalar count over 26–210 MeV: 73,145,594 events. No support-speciﬁc1217
full-data ﬁtted amplitude, local p0, or upper limit enters the selection.1218
Over 26–80 MeV, logistic-turn-on times exponentiated-Chebyshev candidates of degrees four1219
through ten are ﬁtted using only the 10% development spectrum. Degree ﬁve is the lowest candidate1220
passing the frozen source-shape gates: its Poisson deviance per degree of freedom is 0.970 at native1221
binning and 1.141 after ﬁve-bin aggregation, its largest aggregated Pearson pull has magnitude1222
3.521, and no ﬁtted parameter contacts a bound. A quintic C2 smootherstep blends this component1223
into an archived fShiftSigPowTail expectation from 75 to 85 MeV.1224
That broad-tail record has immutable fit_ok=false. An independent static audit nevertheless1225
veriﬁes a ﬁnite nonnegative curve, Pearson χ2/N dof = 0 . 990, free parameters strictly inside their1226
declared bounds, a positive 85–210 MeV tail, and bitwise reproduction of all 100 stored Poisson1227
spectra. The nonconverged ROOT status is explicitly waived only for a smooth broad-tail com-1228
ponent of this conditional source-conditioned stress truth. Neither the component nor the hybrid1229
mean is thereby qualiﬁed as a physical background truth or coverage generator.1230
5.12.2 Frozen Phase-1 rule and outcome1231
The same 25 Poisson spectra are paired across all seven edges. At 44, 49, 54, and 59 MeV, each1232
edge is tested with matched-reference injections of 0, 2, and 5 σA. The 65 MeV holdout is forbidden1233
from support and optimizer-branch selection. Branches are selected using covariance validity, kernel1234
state, reproducibility, and maximum GP log marginal likelihood only.1235
An eligible edge must have complete ﬁnite numerical products, no accepted kernel-bound con-1236
tact, at least 9 of 12 cell means below 0.75 in absolute value including at least 3 of 4 background-only1237
cells, and no cell mean at or above 1.25 in magnitude. Qualifying edges would be ranked by their1238
worst absolute cell mean, with a 0.10 tie band and the smaller tied edge retained. Upper-limit1239
strength cannot rank an edge. The frozen protocol requires the study to stop without retuning1240
when no edge passes Phase 1.1241

5.13 Robustness of the earlier 65 MeV local excess1258
The support study in Section 5.11 changes the background prescription used to interpret the ob-1259
served 2021 spectrum. We compare the v4.2 result (40–300 MeV support) with v4.9.5 (36–300 MeV1260
support). This is a post-unblinding robustness audit, not an additional support-selection criterion.1261
Both scans use the same observed ROOT histogram, mass resolution, signal normalization, signed1262
extraction, and 201 integer-MeV hypotheses.1263
In Figure 72, the v4.2 maximum at 65 MeV changes from Zlocal = 4. 25249 to 2.40288; the new1264
maximum is 2.81029 at 78 MeV. The response is not a uniform suppression: 64 values decrease,1265
60 are unchanged within 10− 12, and 77 increase, with median change zero. The feature did not1266
disappear—the observed counts are unchanged. Its attribution to a narrow signal is not robust to1267
the GP-support prescription.1268

Support-driven kernel migration. We reconstruct four ﬁxed-hyperparameter ﬁts by applying1273
each archived kernel state to each support geometry without reoptimization. On the 40–300 MeV1274
geometry, the new state lowers Z from 4.252 to 2.854. On the 36–300 MeV geometry, the old state1275
would give Z = 4 . 740, while the new state gives 2.403. Within each geometry, the corresponding1276
diagonal state has the larger GP marginal likelihood: 1648.8 versus 1641.9 on the old geometry,1277
and 1676.0 versus 1667.7 on the new one. Thus the support change migrates the preferred GP1278
correlation structure; the eﬀect is not merely that of appending low-mass bins while holding the1279
GP ﬁxed.1280
On the common grid in panel C, the new diagonal state predicts 1949 more events per 0.625 MeV1281
target bin at 65 MeV and 23484 more events over the common ±2. 25σm window (0.1594%). Because1282
the covariance and bin geometry also change, this is not a one-bin subtraction from ˆA; it shows1283
that the new GP accounts for more of the local structure and leaves a smaller narrow-signal ﬁt.1284
Rebin phase, optimizer lineage, and scope. Five native 0.125 MeV bins make each 0.625 MeV1285
coarse bin. Shifting the support by 4 MeV therefore moves the rebin phase by 0.25 MeV. The old1286
65 MeV window spans 60.000–70.000 MeV in 16 bins; the new window spans 60.375–69.750 MeV1287
in 15. Support extent and rebin phase are consequently not isolated. Separating them requires a1288
ﬁxed-anchor rebin study; the counterfactual is a support-geometry test.1289
Branch repair is not the cause. The raw and accepted old 65 MeV states diﬀer by only 0.0911290
ﬁtted event and 1. 7× 10− 5 in Z; v4.9.5 repairs 94, 152, and 212 MeV, not 65 MeV. The often-quoted1291
old Z = 4. 657 is the 2015+2021 pair, not the all-three result ( Z = 3. 993). Controlled swaps of only1292
the 2021 block reduce these to 2.881 and 2.561. They identify the dataset carrying the change but1293
are not oﬃcial combined scans. These single-mass block swaps do not substitute for a fully rebuilt1294
v4.9.7 scan; Section 6.14 explains why that scan was not authorized.1295
All quoted p0 and Z values are local and asymptotic. The ﬁxed-state and combined-block swaps1296
have no look-elsewhere or model-selection calibration. The support toys are conditional diagnostics,1297

Diagnostic only: p0 and Z are local/asymptotic. Support x kernel and combined-scope swaps are controlled counterfactuals, not trials-corrected significance or coverage calibration.

6 Historical accepted result states1304
The ﬁrst results in this section preserve the version 4.2 combination as a historical reference. They1305
are followed by the separate v4.9.5 2021-only scan and the v4.9.7 downstream gate. Version 4.9.71306
does not recompute the combination because its full-2016 support study returns no provisional1307
edge. Version 4.2 adopts the v4.1 factor-12 upper length-scale setting for the full 2016 sample1308
and completes the corresponding three-campaign result. The full 2015 and 2016 samples and the1309
reviewed 2021 10% sample enter one shared- ε2 likelihood. Relative to v4, only the 2016 resolution-1310
scaled upper factor changes, from 8 to 12. The data, mass grids, ﬁt supports, blind and training1311
exclusions, lower factors, likelihood, and 90% asymptotic CL s construction are otherwise unchanged.1312
The v4.2 products comprise the combined observed limit and its principal 300-pseudoexperiment1313
conditional ensemble, the three campaign-level observed limits, auxiliary 100-pseudoexperiment1314
standalone and pairwise conditional ensembles, and ﬁxed-mass asymptotic p0 scans. The auxiliary1315
bands are evaluated from the reviewed campaign states and are not reassigned from the three-1316
campaign ensemble.1317

Table 14: Data samples, search intervals, GP ﬁt supports, and resolution-scaled length-scale factors
used for the v4.2 results. The factor-12 setting for full 2016 is the accepted outcome of the v4.1

At a mass covered by more than one campaign, the simultaneous likelihood proﬁles one common1319
ε2. Campaign-speciﬁc luminosity, radiative fraction, resolution, eﬃciency, and branching inputs1320
map that parameter to separate expected signal yields. The combined curve is therefore a direct1321
shared-coupling result rather than a pointwise envelope or a luminosity-scaled earlier limit. The1322
minimal-visible branching correction above the dimuon threshold is applied consistently to every1323
curve.1324

142 of 142 at factor 8 to 56 of 142 at factor 10 and zero at factors 12, 15, and 20.1328

6.3 Exact reconstruction and conditional ensemble1341
The reviewed state ledger contains exactly 415 campaign–mass GP states: 72 for 2015, 142 for1342
2016, and 201 for 2021. The ﬁnal runner reconstructs the GP mean and covariance at those exact1343
optimized coordinates and evaluates a shared-coupling count-scale likelihood on the union of 2321344
mass hypotheses. Every reported limit and local asymptotic p0 is evaluated at an actual ﬁtted1345
state; no mass point, GP coordinate, limit, or signiﬁcance is interpolated.1346
The combined expected distribution uses 300 conditional background-only pseudoexperiments1347
at each mass. Within a mass, a common toy index joins the campaign-speciﬁc draws entering the1348
simultaneous likelihood. Diﬀerent masses are generated independently, so these are mass-local limit1349
pseudoexperiments, not 300 scan-wide toy spectra. Each pseudoexperiment holds the reviewed1350
GP hyperparameters ﬁxed, without a GP reﬁt, and uses the same asymptotic inner 90% CL s1351
construction as the observed limit. This ensemble supports descriptive combined-limit quantiles1352
and mass-local limit-tail comparisons; it does not establish direct CL s coverage or a scan-wise1353
discovery probability.1354

6.6 Standalone and pairwise conditional limit ensembles1363
An auxiliary ensemble documents the expected ﬁxed-state limit distribution for each standalone1364
campaign and each of the three pairwise shared- ε2 likelihoods. At every mass, it reconstructs1365
the accepted v4.2 300-pseudoexperiment parent stream and selects toy indices 0–99. The same1366
campaign-speciﬁc pseudo-spectrum is therefore reused whenever that campaign appears at a com-1367
mon toy index. The 415 standalone campaign–mass rows and 224 genuine two-campaign overlap1368
rows contain exactly 100 ﬁnite upper limits each, for 63,900 evaluated toy limits. The reviewed GP1369
state is held ﬁxed in every pseudoexperiment.1370

The comparison shows that the localized feature is predominantly carried by the 2021 10% sam-1394
ple and is modestly supported by 2015. The 2016 spectrum does not favor the signal yield implied1395
by the common coupling and lowers the simultaneous signiﬁcance relative to the 2021 standalone1396
value. The largest absolute shared-ﬁt Pearson residual in any one displayed bin is approximately1397
2.93; the combined Zlocal = 3. 99321 is instead a correlated multi-bin proﬁle-likelihood result. These1398
plots document an apparent localized excess on the selected ﬁxed card. They do not supply a scan-1399
toy global signiﬁcance, and the 300 mass-local limit pseudoexperiments do not calibrate such a1400
claim.1401

6.12 Relation of the v4.9.1 validation to the accepted result1427
The background-validation consolidation in Section 5 changes the qualiﬁcation record, not the1428
numerical curves in this section. At 65 MeV the historical 1% × 10 and native-10% toy cells are1429
replaced by explicitly labelled threshold-aware full-100 ensembles. Their zero-signal mean pulls1430
are 0.1394 with 90% interval [ −0. 0499, 0.3288] and −0. 2463 with interval [ −0. 4165, −0. 0761]. Both1431
intervals are wholly inside the post-result ±0. 5 practical band, and all 20 zero-signal and all 801432
lane–mass–strength point means lie within |p|< 0. 5. The practical background-model-mean-bias1433
endpoint is therefore complete for that conditional composite suite.1434
This evidence is narrower than a statement about exact calibration or the observed spectrum.1435
3/20 zero-signal and 15/80 all-strength width intervals exclude one, and the paired response is stable1436
at 96.6–96.9%, corresponding to 3.1–3.4% attenuation. The native-10%, 65 MeV replacement uses1437
a distinct 30–300 MeV validation scheme because the historical truth did not adequately model1438
the rising turn-on. Its threshold-focused source ﬁt meaningfully tests local behavior of the full-1439
support extraction, but the ﬁxed-hyperparameter tail-removal diagnostic shows a nonzero aggregate1440
contribution from bins above 150 MeV. No observed value, expected band, local p0, or limit in this1441
results section is changed or selected by the v4.9.1 toys.1442
6.13 Historical v4.9.5 observed 2021 native-10% scan with its selected support1443
The native 2021 10% histogram was scanned only after the 36–300 MeV support had been ﬁxed.1444
Relative to the v4.2 2021 conﬁguration, the scan changes that support, selects the 2021 input1445
alone, and omits expected-band and toy-calibration products. At each of the 201 integer-MeV1446
hypotheses, the proﬁled asymptotic ˜qµ construction gives an observed 90% CL s signal-yield limit1447
and the corresponding ε2 limit. The signed proﬁle likelihood also gives a local analytic p0.1448
The initial optimizer stopped at its starting state at 94, 152, and 212 MeV. Each of those masses1449
was rerun without changing the analysis card. We retained the highest-log-marginal-likelihood1450
branch reproduced by at least two attempts; no agreement in the ﬁtted signal, limit, or p-value was1451
used, and no point was interpolated. The repaired result contains 201 ﬁnite rows, no unchanged1452
starting states, complete density support, and no kernel-bound contacts.1453
The observed yield limits range from 2287 to 28077 events, while the ε2 limits range from 1. 41 ×1454
10− 6 to 2. 11 × 10− 5. The smallest local analytic value is p0 = 0. 00247 at 78 MeV, equivalent to Z =1455
2. 81 under the local asymptotic mapping. This is a local diagnostic, not a global signiﬁcance: the1456
scan has not been calibrated with background-only pseudoexperiments or another trials correction.1457
This pass also provides no expected-limit bands or direct CL s coverage result.1458

The requested v4.9.7 recombination was conditional on a successful full-2016 support freeze. Sec-1460

record therefore denies the Phase-2 continuation, the support-speciﬁc full-2016 observed scan, the1462
65 MeV holdout, and the combined 2015-full + 2016-full + 2021-10% likelihood.1463

The release retains fail-closed observed and combined workﬂow source as unexecuted scaﬀolding.1464
It does not contain a combined card, a reviewed 2016 state ledger, a cache-closure product, band1465

7 Conclusions1473
Version 4.9.7 applies a frozen, pull-blind two-stage support protocol to full-2016 inference. Phase 11474
tests lower edges from 28 through 33 MeV, plus a 34 MeV geometry control, using 25 paired1475
conditional backgrounds. No edge passes: each has only 3 of 12 cell means below 0.75 in magnitude,1476
only 1 of 4 background-only means below 0.75, and a worst mean pull between 2.281 and 2.696. The1477
30 and 32 MeV lanes additionally contain one retained irreproducible reﬁt each. No support-speciﬁc1478
full-data ﬁtted amplitude, local p0, or upper limit enters this decision.1479
The failure is conditional on the generating construction. The pre-existing 2016 10% develop-1480
ment sample/subset supplies partial observed-shape information, while the full-2016 array enters1481
before the decision only through one scalar normalization over 26–210 MeV. The hybrid mean also1482
uses an explicitly waived broad-tail continuation whose archived ROOT record has fit_ok=false.1483
Its favorable Pearson statistic, ﬁnite positive shape, in-bound parameters, and bitwise toy repro-1484
duction do not turn it into a qualiﬁed physical truth or coverage generator.1485
A uniform post-decision ﬁt of the unﬂuctuated analytic mean shows the same mass-dependent1486
mismatch at every edge: roughly +2σA at 49 MeV, −2. 6σA at 54 MeV, and +3. 5σA at 59 MeV.1487
This conﬁrms that the observed Phase-1 pattern is not merely a Poisson ﬂuctuation.1488
The predeclared stop action therefore authorizes no downstream production. No provisional sup-1489
port, Phase 2 conﬁrmation, 65 MeV holdout, support-speciﬁc full-2016 observed scan, or reviewed1490
2016 state ledger is present.1491
94

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
The v4.9.7 combined upper limit and 100-toy combined band are also absent by construction.1492
Choosing the numerically smallest failing edge would be a retrospective rule change and is not1493
done.1494
The separate 2021 robustness audit resolves the apparent “disappearance” of the earlier 65 MeV1495
signal-like feature more precisely. The observed counts are unchanged, but the ﬁtted narrow-signal1496
coeﬃcient falls by 39.0% and its local asymptotic Z changes from 4.252 to 2.403 when the GP-1497
support prescription changes. The support change shifts the coarse-rebin phase and migrates the1498
preferred GP kernel state; the new continuum is higher through the masked region. Optimizer1499
repair and signal normalization do not explain the change. The defensible conclusion is that the1500
narrow-signal attribution is not robust to the support prescription—not that the feature has been1501
proved background or that signal has been excluded.1502
The v4.2 three-campaign combination and v4.9.5 2021-only scan remain preserved as distinct1503
historical states. The unexecuted v4.9.7 observed and combined workﬂow source is retained only1504
to document the gate that prevented production. A future combined result requires a separately1505
named, prospectively speciﬁed validation protocol or an explicitly chosen historical-support fallback.1506
Neither is inferred from this failed support scan.1507
A Version 4.9 Threshold-Support Qualiﬁcation Record1508
Disposition: numerically complete; production freeze not supported. The full
paired-support extraction is complete and reproducible. The source truth is globally unqual-
iﬁed and the primary support fails six of eight near-threshold zero-signal cells, so numerical
completion does not promote the candidate card.
1509
A.1 Frozen scope and cardinality1510
Each of the 1% × 10 and native-10% source families contributes 25 independent Poisson spectra1511
generated over 30–300 MeV. Both the 30–300 and 40–300 MeV GP supports are applied to the1512
same cached counts. Four masses (55, 60, 65, and 70 MeV) and four signed injection strengths1513
(z = 0 , 1, 3, 5) give 2 × 25 × 4 × 4 = 800 states per support and 1600 total. The supports have1514
identical key inventories. Source families are independent; masses, injections, and supports within1515
a source family are correlated through their shared backgrounds.1516
The analysis preserves the frozen v4.2/v4.5 settings other than the support lower edge. No1517
expected-limit toys, observed limits, exclusions, or scan-maximum calibrations are produced.1518
A.2 Source-truth construction and adverse gate1519
The executed truth label is fSigPowExpQ-anchored residual stress truth . For each source, a1520
degree-six logistic-times-exponentiated-Chebyshev model is ﬁtted on 30–80 MeV. A ﬁxed C2 blend1521
from 75 to 85 MeV joins that local component to an identiﬁable fSigPowExpQ anchor, which supplies1522
the tail through 300 MeV. The truth is positive and exactly normalized to the corresponding 30–1523
300 MeV source total.1524
The local native-bin deviance/ndf values are 1.010 and 1.140 for the 1% and native-10% sources.1525
Those values do not override the adverse global checks: the native-10% 85–300 MeV tail de-1526
viance/ndf is 6.22, and the largest leave-one- 2. 25σm-window- out drift is 4.84%. All sixteen1527
pure fSigPowExpQ/fGenGammaThresh ﬁts over the predeclared 30/40 to 100/150 MeV ranges have1528
fit_ok=false. Thus neither a pure simple family nor the executed joined truth is promoted as an1529
independent globally qualiﬁed generator.1530

A.5 Allowed claims1533
The study establishes a statistically resolved, same-toy dependence of the 65 MeV conditional pull1534
on the GP lower support edge. It also establishes that the change is not suﬃcient for uniform closure1535
under the executed stress truth. It does not show that the observed 65 MeV feature is background,1536
that GPR coverage is calibrated, or that the 30–300 MeV production support is qualiﬁed.1537
The proposed restricted-range asterisk is retained only as guidance for a future study: a1538
threshold-faithful, source-qualiﬁed function evaluated on 30–100 or 30–150 MeV could demonstrate1539
a local threshold-modeling requirement without changing the observed-data support. No restricted1540
toy ensemble was executed in v4.9, and the simple families tested on those ranges failed before1541
extraction.1542
B Archived Validation Batteries Through Version 4.81543
Findings ﬁrst. The v4.6 full-100 study conﬁrmed that the 1% × 10 65 MeV oﬀset persists
beyond its ten-toy pilot. The v4.7 threshold-speciﬁc truth reduced that oﬀset but did not give
uniform closure in 1% × 10 and native 10%. The v4.8 residual-structured means failed their
source and signal-inﬂuence qualiﬁcation gates; their completed extractions remain conditional
stress tests only. These studies motivate v4.9 but do not contribute rows to its 25-background
ensemble and do not change the accepted v4.2 result.

B.2 Validation of the Statistical Framework1552
The closure tests in this section validate the local extraction and are not themselves conﬁdence-1553
level statements. Some historical reach proxies use the one-sided 95% Gaussian coeﬃcient, while1554
the current physics-facing result in Section 6 is a 90% CL upper limit obtained with the CL s1555
construction. The staged suites shown here are the historical validation basis inherited from v3:1556
the fully unblinded 2015 sample, the 2016 10% development sample, and the regenerated 20211557
1% validation sample. Their headline functional-form ﬁgures use full GP reﬁts and, for 2021, the1558
corrected matched-reference widened-window convention. They do not constitute support-matched1559
closure for the v4 full-2016 and 2021-10% state; that remaining gate is stated with the v4 result.1560
Superseded and duplicated closure displays are collected in Appendix C.1561
The validation material is organized around four checks of analysis robustness:1562
• First, how far from the signal peak should the GP sideband training data begin?1563
100

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
• Second, after that training exclusion is ﬁxed, should the 1. 64– 2. 25 σm signal tails be omitted as1564
a guard band or included in the extraction likelihood through a wider blind window?1565
• Third, do the reference-matched full-reﬁt toys close for the 2015, 2016, and 2021 datasets?1566
• Finally, do the combined pseudoexperiments accumulate sensitivity as expected when statisti-1567
cally independent datasets share one coupling parameter?1568
T erms used in the validation plots1569
train-k or ktrain The GP sideband ﬁt excludes bins with |m − m0|< k σm around the tested mass.1570
The selected production value is train- 2. 25, matched to the 2. 25 σm blind/extraction window.1571
refmatched The injected amplitude is deﬁned with the same background-only reﬁt reference scale,1572
blind geometry, kernel policy, and reﬁt prescription used in the extraction study: Ainj =1573
ZinjσA, ref .1574
Zinj The nominal injected signal strength in units of σA, ref .1575
ˆZ The extracted signal signiﬁcance, usually summarized as ˆA/σ A for toy-closure plots.1576
pull mean The ensemble mean of ( ˆA − Ainj)/σ A. A value near zero indicates little bias.1577
pull width The ensemble standard deviation of the pull. A value near one indicates that the ﬁtted1578
σA is correctly calibrated.1579
∆ Z The signiﬁcance residual ˆZ − Zinj.1580
Gaussian-equivalent expected reach proxy The background-only proxy 1. 64 σA, ref /A ε2=11581
used to compare window choices. It is a compact expected-95% scale, not the ﬁnal CL s limit.1582
Throughout this section, the zero-injection row has a special interpretation. At Zinj = 0 no1583
signal is added, so the pull mean, pull width, and ∆ Z = ˆZ − Zinj form a spurious-signal test.1584
Centered pulls and near-zero ∆ Z mean that the GP-plus-template extraction is not manufacturing1585
a signal in a smooth background-only spectrum. Nonzero injected rows then test whether a real1586
signal is recovered after the same sideband-reﬁt prescription has been applied.1587
B.3 Training exclusion and blind-window width1588
The ﬁrst decision is the training exclusion: how far from the test mass the sideband bins used by1589
the GP should begin. Figure 89 illustrates the adopted wide-blind geometry. For a large injected1590
signal, Gaussian tails remain visible outside the central peak. The training-exclusion studies below1591
show why training directly next to a narrow 1. 64 σm extraction window is unattractive: those tails1592
can be oﬀered to the GP as ordinary smooth-background information and then be partly absorbed1593
by the reﬁtted background.1594
101

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
Figure 89: Illustration of the selected 2. 25 σm blind-window construction for a large injected signal.
The GP is trained only outside the widened blinded region, so the near tails of a Gaussian resonance
are not included in the sideband-training data. The same blinded region is then used by the local
signal-extraction likelihood.
The training-exclusion scan starts from the older 1. 64 σm blind window and varies the outer1595
boundary of the bins excluded from GP training. In this notation, train- k means that bins with1596
|m − m0|< k σm are withheld from the GP sideband ﬁt. The train- 2. 25 choice is not the point with1597
the strongest raw background-only reach proxy; it is selected from the combined leakage, recovery,1598
and calibration evidence below.1599
In the comparison of diﬀerent blind windows shown in Figure 90, train- 1. 96 gives the lowest1600
median Gaussian-equivalent ε2
95 proxy, but it also leaves a much larger fraction of the injected signal1601
template in the GP training region, leading to higher leakage rate and lower signal sensitivity. The1602
train-2. 25 exclusion is the balanced candidate: it sharply reduces train-region leakage, is more1603
sensitive to injected signal than 1. 96, and retains better expected reach than the wider 2. 58 and 3. 01604
exclusions. The compact evidence is summarized in Table 18; for the train- 2. 25 choice, the mean1605
signal recovery is about 0. 961 at Zinj = 3 and 0. 943 at Zinj = 5, with a maximum injected training1606
fraction of about 0. 0258.1607

can feed signal-like counts to the GP sideband ﬁt. Table 18 then gives the compact numerical trade-1612

left in the training region.1614
The remaining guard-band ﬁgures separate the ingredients behind that table. Figure 90 shows1615
the cost side of widening the training exclusion: as nearby sideband bins are removed, σA, ref grows1616
and the Gaussian-equivalent expected reach softens. Figure 91 shows the protection side: train- 2. 251617
greatly reduces the signal fraction available to the GP training set while preserving the matched1618
reference scale used to deﬁne the injected amplitudes. Figure 92 checks that this protection does not1619
create a new calibration problem by comparing recovery, ∆ Z, pull means, and pull widths over the1620
mass and injection grid. Figure 93 then shows the same recovery behavior at the toy-distribution1621
level, making the narrow-guard under-recovery visible beyond the ensemble mean curves. Finally,1622
Figures 94– 98 compare the two blind-window geometries after train- 2. 25 is ﬁxed: expected reach1623
favors including the 1. 64– 2. 25 σm tails in the likelihood, while the background-only, pull-summary,1624
amplitude-recovery, and ∆ Z panels show that the widened blind window keeps the same closure1625
behavior as the explicit guard-band construction.1626

B.4 Three toy-generation modes1637
The validation program uses three complementary pseudoexperiment constructions. Analytic1638
functional-form pseudoexperiments provide the required smooth-background closure test because1639
the true toy spectrum is independent of the GP model later used in the extraction. Conditional1640
blind-window GP toys are not used as a standalone closure requirement; they are a fast diagnostic1641
of the local extraction likelihood when the sideband-trained GP prediction is held ﬁxed. Full pro-1642
cedural GP-mean/reﬁt pseudoexperiments are the required closure stress test for signal absorption,1643
because each toy is generated over the full mass range and then passed back through the same1644
sideband-reﬁt and extraction chain as the data.1645
B.4.1 Analytic functional-form pseudoexperiments1646
The ﬁrst class of toys starts from an analytic ﬁt to the smooth e+e− invariant-mass spectrum1647
and ﬂuctuates pseudo-data around that closed-form background model. These studies isolate the1648
question of gross spectral smoothness from the later question of GP conditioning. They are therefore1649
useful as the ﬁrst closure test before the full inference chain is exercised.1650
The primary analytic seeds are written explicitly here because their role is to deﬁne smooth
positive toy means, not to claim a unique physical parameterization of the trident continuum. With
S(m; mt, w ) = [1+exp {−(m− mt)/w }]− 1 and ud = (m− mmid,d )/m scale,d , the three selected dataset
seeds are

The 2015 and 2016 functions are independent ﬁts of the same shifted sigmoid-power-exponential1651
family with a normalized quadratic tail factor; they are set to zero below the ﬁtted turn-on oﬀset1652
m0,d and are useful when the low-mass turn-on is better described by a shifted variable. The 20211653
seed uses the unshifted sigmoid-power-exponential form with an exponential quadratic modiﬁer,1654
which was preferred for the 2021 1% spectrum because the wider support and smoother low-mass1655
turn-on did not require the shifted threshold parameter.1656
The ﬁt selection algorithm is implemented in a deliberately conservative ROOT macro. For each1657
dataset the macro builds a small list of positive candidate families, scans the conﬁgured lower edge1658
of the ﬁt interval, and for each candidate tries several seeded minimizations covering early turn-on,1659
broad-tail, and late-turn-on shapes. Each trial is ﬁt to the input histogram over the dataset-speciﬁc1660
ﬁt range with bin-integrated expectations, then evaluated with Pearson and Neyman goodness-of-1661
ﬁt on the selected support and with normalization checks on the full support range, scan range,1662
and low/high sideband fractions. The primary seed is the ﬁrst preferred candidate that has ﬁnite1663
parameters, passes the support and sideband-fraction checks, and satisﬁes the conﬁgured Pearson1664
target; if no preferred candidate passes, the best validated candidate is used. Here, support check1665
means enough sideband bins after mass-dependent exclusion; sideband-fraction check means the1666
retained sideband fraction; and Pearson target means the toy-validation χ2-like residual threshold.1667
113

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
The selected function is then frozen, normalized to the observed number of events on the full1668
toy-support range, and used to generate Poisson pseudo-data bin by bin from the normalized bin1669
integrals.1670
The candidate families were chosen to span the few smooth structures that can mimic the1671
observed spectra without introducing narrow features: a sigmoid threshold factor for turn-on be-1672
havior, a power-law rise, an exponential falloﬀ for the high-mass tail, optional threshold shifts1673
for datasets whose support begins near a sharp turn-on, and low-order exponential curvature for1674
residual broad-shape freedom. The generalized-gamma and simpler sigmoid-power variants in the1675
candidate overlay provide cross-checks that the chosen seed is not an artifact of one algebraic form.1676
They are not used to inject additional model uncertainty in these closure plots; once the primary1677
seed is selected, the closure question is whether the GP extraction recovers known signals from that1678
ﬁxed smooth truth.1679
Figure 99 shows the outcome of this construction rather than an internal parameter dump. In1680
each row, the left panel overlays the input histogram with all enabled candidate families, while1681
the right panel compares the selected seed with the pseudoexperiment-ensemble mean. The 20151682
shifted-tail family follows both the turn-on and falling spectrum without introducing a narrow1683
feature. The 2016 row shows the same family on the 10% sample. The 2021 row selects the1684
unshifted fSigPowExpQ family, whose broad curvature follows the 1% spectrum without requiring1685
a shifted threshold. Most importantly, each right-hand panel demonstrates that the Poisson toy1686
mean reproduces the selected analytic expectation and that the toy normalization is set on the1687
stated support rather than ﬁxed inside the search window.1688
Injected-signal lower-bound scans. The lower-bound choice was made with signal-injection1689
studies, not from the observed limit curve. For every mass and kmin setting, the analytic background1690
was Poisson ﬂuctuated, a full-range Gaussian signal was injected, the GP was reﬁt to the toy1691
sidebands, and the blinded-window extraction was repeated. The 2015 and 2016 grids used Zinj =1692
0, 1, 2, 3, 5 at masses 45 MeV, 60 MeV, 75 MeV, 90 MeV and 105 MeV and 60 MeV, 90 MeV, 105 MeV,1693
120 MeV and 150 MeV, respectively. The corrected 2021 grid used Zinj = 0, 1, 3, 5 at 50 MeV, 60 MeV,1694
90 MeV, 120 MeV, 180 MeV and 210 MeV. There are 100 toys per 2015 mass–strength cell and 251695
toys per 2016 and 2021 cell. The zero-injection cells test for a ﬁtted false signal; the nonzero cells1696
test recovery, ﬁtted uncertainty, and signiﬁcance calibration across the injected-strength range.1697
Table 19 makes the mean-pull information explicit. The signed Zinj = 0 entry is the equal-1698
weight mean of the background-only cell means over the tested masses. The signed Zinj > 0 entry1699
is the equal-weight mean over all nonzero mass–strength cells. The RMS column is the root mean1700
square of those nonzero cell means and is sensitive to biases that change sign across the grid; the1701
last column is the median nonzero-injection pull width. No uncertainty is assigned to the aggregate1702
rows because the plots retain the cell-by-cell toy-derived uncertainties used to judge whether a1703
pattern is coherent.1704

B.4.2 Conditional blind-window GP toys1731
The fastest toy mode keeps the sideband-trained GP ﬁxed at a given test mass. The GP ﬁt is1732
performed once on the sidebands, the blind-window mean vector and covariance are constructed1733
from that ﬁt, a background realization is drawn, signal is added, and the result is Poisson-sampled.1734
This construction is ideal for high-statistics expected-band studies and for testing the response of1735
the extraction ﬁt under the posterior background model preferred by the real data.1736
B.4.3 Full procedural GP-mean/reﬁt pseudoexperiments1737
The most realistic toy mode generates a full pseudoexperiment from the GP-derived background1738
expectation, injects signal with the full Gaussian template, and then reruns the GP sideband-1739
to-blind prediction on the toy before signal extraction. Here “GP mean” means the full-range1740
propagated count-space mean obtained from the sideband-ﬁt GP model of the observed spectrum.1741
That propagated mean is used as the toy-truth background source over the full histogram support;1742
the toy is then ﬂuctuated, signal is added, and the ordinary mass-scan workﬂow reﬁts the GP1743
on the toy sidebands at each scanned mass. This is the appropriate mode for diagnosing signal1744
absorption, because it directly tests how much of the injected signal may be absorbed by the reﬁtted1745
background model or by the proﬁled nuisance treatment. The phrase “reﬁt-on-toy” is therefore1746
literal: the search stage does not reuse the original data-conditioned blind-window prediction.1747
B.4.4 Fixed-total GP-propagated toy-scan study1748
The GP-propagated ﬁxed-total toy mode remains a useful internal cross-check of the scan machinery,1749
but it is not the main closure evidence in this note. The main text therefore emphasizes the clean1750
year-matched functional-form scan suites above and the refmatched full-reﬁt closure studies below.1751
B.5 Full-100 2021 source-and-exposure refmatched closure1752
Finding summary . The complete ensemble conﬁrms a local conditional 65 MeV oﬀset in the1753
1% × 10 lane, with a smaller native-10% response, after a pull-blind optimizer treatment. It does1754
not establish observed-data bias or direct coverage.1755
The ten-toy hyperparameter pilot and its ﬁrst refmatched continuation are now archived in1756
Appendix H. This section uses the complete 100-toy ensembles. It was frozen before the reserve1757
toys were inspected: toys 0–9 are the pilot sample and toys 10–99 are an independent conﬁrmation1758
sample for the pilot-selected 1% × 10, 65 MeV zero-signal oﬀset. All 100 background pseudo-1759
histograms are reﬁtted uniformly under the new estimator and restart policy; the v4.5 extraction1760
rows are not pooled with the new rows.1761
The analysis card remains the declared v4.2 2021 state: a 40–300 MeV GP support, 50–250 MeV1762
search range, kmin = 1. 1, kmax = 15, a 2. 25σm blind and training exclusion, two required sidebands,1763
ﬁve-bin local rebinning, and 12 randomized optimizer restarts in addition to the nominal start.1764
The four headline ensembles are 1% × 10, native 10%, 1% × 100, and 10% × 10. The common1765
fGenGammaThresh family is ﬁtted independently to the native 1% and native 10% spectra. These1766
functions are smooth stress truths, not physical background generators. In particular, the native-1767
10% ﬁt has Pearson χ2/ ndf = 2 . 781 and is deliberately not retuned after inspecting closure.1768
For each scenario s, background toy t, and mass m, an independently optimized matched1769
background-only ﬁt deﬁnes σref
A,stm . The injected amplitude and reported pull are1770

B.5.1 Predeclared optimizer-repeat and exclusion gate1776
Each optimizer attempt reﬁts exactly the same cached background and signal counts; the pseudoex-1777
periment seed and optimizer-start seed occupy separate deterministic namespaces. An attempt uses1778
the nominal kernel start plus 12 randomized starts and archives its seed, warnings, log marginal1779
likelihood (LML), length scale, constant, extraction uncertainty, and covariance diagnostics. Three1780
independently seeded, identically conﬁgured background-only/zero-signal optimizer attempts use1781
the same data and masks. The highest-LML branch must occur at least twice under the predeclared1782
agreement criteria1783
|∆LML |
Ntrain
≤ 10− 3, |∆ ln ℓ| ≤0. 01, |∆ ln C| ≤0. 05, |∆ ln σA| ≤0. 02. (86)
If it does not, two additional independent attempts are made. A nonzero-injection ﬁt begins1784
with one attempt and receives two, then at most ﬁve, attempts if it has an invalid or nonﬁ-1785
nite ﬁt/covariance state, retains the exact optimizer-start signature, lies within 2% of a length-1786
scale bound, or has ˆσA/σ ref
A /∈ [0. 5, 2]. Branch selection uses only maximum GP LML and1787
reproducibility—never ˆA, pull sign or size, ˆε2, or agreement with the injected amplitude. Opti-1788
mizer warnings are archived for audit, but are not by themselves a repeat or exclusion trigger; only1789
the pull- and amplitude-blind numerical diagnostics listed above activate the adaptive attempts.1790
An irreproducible reference state excludes all four strengths at that scenario–toy–mass key;1791
an irreproducible injected reﬁt excludes only that strength. Statistical extremes that pass this1792
pull-blind technical gate remain accepted; repeat-triggered boundary solutions are retained when1793
replicated. A closure cell was predeclared reportable only if at least 95 of its 100 generated rows1794
survived. This ﬁt-state policy avoids both post-hoc pull clipping and the scientiﬁcally incorrect1795
removal of a complete pseudoexperiment because it ﬂuctuates unfavorably.1796
The production run contains 400 scenario–toy tasks, 8000 predeclared extraction-state keys,1797
7996 ﬁnite raw amplitude ﬁts, and 12,845 archived optimizer-attempt records. Of the 7994 accepted1798
states, 5593 required one attempt, 2384 required three, and 17 required ﬁve. Six states are excluded:1799
the 1% × 100 toy-83, 90 MeV, z = 3 reﬁt; the 10% × 10 toy-28, 180 MeV, z = 5 reﬁt; and all four1800
strengths of the 10%×10 toy-66, 90 MeV reference state. The ﬁrst two fail injected-ﬁt reproducibility1801
and the last fails reference-branch reproducibility after ﬁve attempts. No entire background-toy1802
index is removed, every one of the 80 closure cells retains 99 or 100 rows, and all pass the predeclared1803
minimum-size gate.1804
The count-space covariance produced by the frozen lognormal-moment mapping is classiﬁed1805
as numerically repairable, not necessarily positive semideﬁnite, when it is ﬁnite and symmetric1806
and satisﬁes λmin/ maxi |Cii| ≥ −0. 01. The extraction likelihood then follows its frozen numerical1807
path: progressively larger diagonal jitter through 10− 3 maxi |Cii|, followed, when needed, by an1808
eigenvalue-clipped square root. Among the 7994 accepted states, 498 archived raw matrices have1809
the eigenvalue ratio below −10− 3 and 22 lie below −0. 005; the latter are conﬁned to 90 MeV in1810

the two high-exposure ensembles. A pull-blind inﬂuence check that omits those 22 rows changes1811
any closure-cell mean by at most 0.031 and any width by at most 0.014, but would reduce one1812
cell below the predeclared 95-row reporting threshold. The rows therefore remain in the headline1813
moments. An all-state deterministic reconstruction replays the actual repair path and compares1814
it with the minimally suﬃcient isotropic diagonal loading. The replay agrees with the archived1815
pull to within 0.0031 and with its uncertainty to within 3. 2 × 10− 4 relatively; changing to minimal1816
diagonal loading moves any pull by at most 0.0029 and any uncertainty by at most 6. 94 × 10− 41817
relatively; the largest changes in a cell mean and width are 5. 0 × 10− 5 and 1. 1 × 10− 4, respectively.1818
The archived ratios, rather than reconstructed threshold counts, deﬁne the 498 and 22 ﬁgures above.1819
This tolerance and repair audit is a numerical qualiﬁcation of the estimator, not evidence that the1820
unrepaired matrices are positive semideﬁnite.1821
B.5.2 Spurious-signal bias and independent conﬁrmation1822
Figure 105 isolates the z = 0 tests. The apparent 1% × 10, 65 MeV pilot oﬀset does not disappear1823
with more toys. In the predeclared independent reserve (toys 10–99), the mean pull is 0. 768 with1824
sample width 0. 944 and 95% Student- t interval [0. 570, 0. 965]; the two-sided test of zero mean gives1825
p = 1 . 64 × 10− 11. This exceeds the predeclared materiality threshold |¯p| ≥ 0. 2. The full-1001826
precision estimate is 0. 789 with width 0. 962, median 0. 784, and 95% interval [0. 598, 0. 980].1827
The deterministic analytic-mean stress spectrum gives a stable zero-signal pull of 0. 837 at the1828
same mass. The agreement with the 100-toy mean and the near-normal reserve quantiles in Fig. 1061829
identify a local functional-form/GP underprediction, not an optimizer failure or a few statistical1830
outliers. The four injection strengths share each background toy and are highly correlated; their1831
similar oﬀsets are recovery diagnostics, not four independent conﬁrmations.1832
The remaining zero-signal cells were treated as exploratory with Holm correction over all 201833
scenario–mass checks. Two additional material oﬀsets survive that correction: native 10% at 65 MeV1834
has mean 0. 716 (analytic-mean value 0. 723), and 10% × 10 at 180 MeV has mean 0. 338 (analytic-1835
mean value 0. 194). These are closure properties conditional on the deliberately imperfect smooth1836
stress truth and the frozen card. They do not measure a bias of the observed data result and do1837
not calibrate frequentist coverage.1838
B.5.3 Injection recovery , pull widths, and signed coupling coordinates1839
Figures 107– 110 use the four-panel convention of Figs. 100– 102. The pull-mean error bars are 95%1840
Student-t intervals and the width bars are exact normal-theory intervals based on the accepted1841
sample size. The lower panels show residual ﬁtted signiﬁcance and median amplitude recovery with1842
empirical central 68% spreads. Joining lines connect only the ﬁve predeclared mass points.1843

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
The full ensemble resolves the principal v4.5 ambiguity. The two spectacular pilot branches were1851
numerical and are repaired by reproducible maximum-LML selection, while the 1% × 10, 65 MeV1852
oﬀset is a stable local closure failure of the declared smooth stress truth under the frozen GP1853
analysis. Only irreproducible numerical ﬁt states are excluded; statistical extremes that pass the1854
pull-blind gate remain in every arithmetic moment. The result is a full-100 conditional injection–1855
extraction closure study, but it is not direct CL s coverage, a scan-wise global-signiﬁcance calibration,1856
or evidence that the observed 65 MeV feature is signal or background. The accepted v4.2 observed1857
result and its conditional limit bands remain unchanged.1858
B.6 Validation of near-threshold background modeling1859
Finding summary . A source-dedicated turn-on truth improves the 65 MeV response for 1% × 101860
but leaves nonuniform oﬀsets in both targeted lanes and neighboring masses. Better threshold1861
modeling is relevant, but this 20-background study does not freeze a support or generator choice.1862
Version 4.6 established that the positive 65 MeV closure response was not explained by a few1863
pseudoexperiments or by the two conspicuous optimizer branches in the v4.5 pilot. Its common1864
fGenGammaThresh stress truth gave full-100 background-only mean pulls of 0.789 for 1% × 10 and1865
0.716 for native 10%, with deterministic analytic-mean pulls of 0.837 and 0.723. The present1866
study tests the more speciﬁc hypothesis that this behavior is caused by inadequate modeling of1867
the 2021 turn-on. It replaces the generating truth, but holds ﬁxed the v4.2 search card and the1868
v4.5 matched-reference injection semantics. In particular, it does not tune the 40 MeV GP-support1869
edge, the 2. 25σm blind/training exclusion, or the 2021 lower length-scale factor using the resulting1870
pull direction.1871
B.6.1 Dedicated turn-on truth and ﬁxed model selection1872
The native 2021 1% and 10% source histograms are ﬁtted independently over 35–80 MeV. For1873
source d and Chebyshev degree p, the candidate intensity in expected counts per GeV is1874

. (87)
Every expected native-bin count is the integral of Eq. ( 87) over the bin, evaluated with ﬁxed order-1875
16 Gauss–Legendre quadrature. Doubling the order changes any expected bin count by at most1876
1. 1 × 10− 13 relatively. Twelve deterministic multistart ﬁts are made for degrees 3, 4, and 5. A1877
candidate passes only if its native-bin Poisson deviance/ndf is at most 1.5, its ﬁve-bin deviance/ndf1878
is at most 2.0, its largest absolute ﬁve-bin Pearson pull is at most 5, and no ﬁtted parameter1879
contacts its declared bound. These criteria use no GP extraction or injected-signal result.1880
The selected local intensity is used directly from 40 to 75 MeV. Between 75 and 80 MeV it is1881
joined to the archived fGenGammaThresh tail with the C2 weight1882
q(u) = u3(10 − 15u + 6u2), u = m − 0. 075
0. 005 , (88)
clipped to zero below 75 MeV and one above 80 MeV. The local and tail intensities, including1883
this weight, are integrated jointly in every bin. A single tail scale preserves the observed native1884
40–300 MeV total before the 1% source is multiplied by ten. The solved coeﬃcients multiplying the1885
archived tail are 0.9997674467 and 0.9998089682 for the 1% and 10% sources. Because the local-to-1886
tail blend also contributes to the total, the resulting new-to-archived bin ratios above 80 MeV are1887
0.987024 and 0.987130. Consequently this is not a strictly local replacement: all bins above 80 MeV1888

B.6.2 Frozen analysis path, ensemble, and numerical gate1897
Exactly 20 independent Poisson background histograms are generated from each source-derived1898
mean: 10λ(1%) and the native 10% mean. Thus there are 40 independent background spectra, not1899
640 independent spectra. Each background is reused at the four masses and four injection strengths,1900
so mass cells are correlated and the four strength cells at ﬁxed mass have pull correlations between1901
0.994 and 1.000. The two source families are ﬁtted and generated independently; their comparison1902
is unpaired and includes both source-shape and normalization diﬀerences.1903
The card retains the 50–250 MeV search range, 40–300 MeV GP support, rebin-ﬁve geome-1904
try, factor-1.1/factor-15 resolution-scaled 2021 length-scale bounds, and 2. 25σm blind and training1905
exclusions. Log preprocessing is applied to both the mass coordinate and every positive training1906
count, with ﬁxed log-space noise variance αi = 1/y i. Every accepted ﬁt has strictly positive training1907
counts: the minimum is 3876 in the 1% × 10 lane and 4486 in native 10%. Table 22 records the1908
exact asymmetric geometry close to the lower support edge.1909

B.6.3 Deterministic and twenty-toy closure results1930
Table 23 compares the deterministic response to the embedded old and new analytic means with1931
the new 20-toy zero-signal ensembles. The intervals on the means are 95% Student- t intervals;1932
widths are sample standard deviations. The old analytic rows are exact same-support diagnostics,1933
but their truth shapes diﬀer from the new generator. The only old Poisson ensemble quoted here1934
is the archived v4.6 100-toy 65 MeV context; it is unpaired with the new toys and cannot by itself1935
establish a causal “before/after” correction.1936
At 65 MeV in the 1%-source-times-ten lane, the new mean is 0.300. This is smaller than1937
the archived v4.6 mean 0.789, whose interval is [0. 598, 0. 980], while the analytic response falls from1938
0.837 to 0.252. This is evidence of partial mitigation conditional on the two declared truths. Native1939
10%, however, changes only from the unpaired v4.6 mean 0. 716 [0. 512, 0. 920] to 0. 641 [0. 082, 1. 199],1940
while its analytic response changes from 0.723 to 0.563. In addition, both new-truth scenarios have1941
negative 55 MeV means and 1% × 10 has a positive 70 MeV mean. The sign reversals of the old and1942
new analytic responses at 55 and 60 MeV show that the inferred spurious signal is strongly truth-1943
shape dependent. The dedicated turn-on form therefore does not provide uniform near-threshold1944
closure or a unique explanation of the observed spectrum.1945
The nonzero injections preserve the same mass pattern. Table 24 gives all four accepted mean1946
pulls and the width range over injection strengths. The full cell-wise 95% intervals are displayed1947
in Fig. 118. Widths range from 0.644 to 1.196, but with only 20 backgrounds their normal-theory1948
intervals are broad; the study neither establishes calibrated unit width nor direct coverage. Because1949
each row reuses one background stream, the similar strength dependence is one correlated recovery1950
diagnostic rather than four independent conﬁrmations.1951

B.6.4 Interpretation and next validation steps1963
The study answers the narrow functional-form question but does not establish a uniform ﬁx. Mod-1964
eling the turn-on directly reduces the 1% × 10 65 MeV response, while native 10% remains positive1965
and other masses expose responses of comparable or larger magnitude. Because the dedicated truth1966
is ﬁtted to the same source spectra, this is conditional model stress testing, not an unbiased mea-1967
surement of the estimator bias on future data. The unpaired old and new ensembles also prevent1968
a causal attribution to the truth replacement alone.1969
The observed geometry suggests several separately frozen follow-ups. At 55 MeV only 17 re-1970
binned training bins lie below the mask and 385 lie above it, so a modestly raised lower support1971
can be tested by cropping the cached toys. A lower-support extension cannot reuse zeros that were1972
imposed below 40 MeV: it requires newly generated sub-40-MeV bins from a validated expanded1973
truth, while holding every bin at or above 40 MeV identical to the reference toys. A one-at-a-1974
time scan of the 65 MeV blind/training half-width, followed if needed by the previously proposed1975
kmin, 2021 = 1 . 0 lower length-scale test, should use identical background and signal streams and1976
the v4.7.1 pull-blind repeat gate. Since nominally small width changes can leave the discrete bin1977
masks unchanged, the ﬁrst tested alternatives should be the discretely eﬀective 2.20 and 2.51 σm1978
half-widths, with the extraction and training masks recorded separately. None should be selected by1979
whichever setting moves the 65 MeV mean closest to zero; fake-gap and signal-injection performance1980
across the complete 55–70 MeV grid must be reported together.1981
More structural alternatives are also justiﬁed. A nonzero parametric turn-on mean followed by1982
a GP residual ﬁt would separate the known boundary trend from residual smooth variation, while1983
a nonstationary or input-warped covariance could allow the correlation scale to change through1984
the turn-on. These are standard GP modeling choices rather than interpretations of the present1985
residuals [24, 25, 32]. If several defensible analytic turn-on families survive those gates, their choice1986
can instead be carried as a discrete-proﬁled nuisance, conditional on validating the family set and1987
penalty prescription [ 42]. Whichever family is considered should pass source-independent or held-1988
out fake-gap tests and a predeclared spurious-signal requirement before observed inference; analo-1989
gous resonance searches explicitly use spurious-signal tests to qualify background parameterizations1990
[43]. The present 20-toy diagnostic is too small and too correlated to supply that qualiﬁcation. It1991
changes no accepted v4.2 card, observed limit, conditional band, local p0, or interpretation of the1992
observed 65 MeV feature.1993

B.7 Residual-structured 2021 generating-mean stress tests1994
F AILED QUALIFICA TION — REQUESTED CONDITIONAL STRESS ONLY.
Both residual-structured means fail the frozen source-qualiﬁcation and signal-inﬂuence gates.
The two 20-background extraction ensembles are complete, but completion does not rescue
either failure. The ﬁxed-knot native-1% lane is additionally upper-bound-censored at the
pilot’s factor-25 fallback. A separate factor-50 ceiling check passes for that targeted lane but
does not rerun or uncensor the closure. Nothing in this subsection is physical background
truth, observed-data bias, coverage, an expected band, a limit, an exclusion, or authority to
change the production card.
1995
B.7.1 Post-exploratory construction and frozen claim boundary1996
The residual patterns exposed by the preceding v4.8 study motivated two alternative generating1997
means. This is therefore post-exploratory model development, not a prospectively blind choice.1998
The model structures and the source-only knot-selection rule were frozen before the signal-inﬂuence1999
audit and before any GPR extraction result from this study was inspected. No pull, recovery, local2000
signiﬁcance, conﬁdence limit, or length-scale response enters the model choice.2001
The ﬁrst candidate is a natural spline in log yield relative to the frozen v4.8 rigid threshold2002
mean. The two-knot candidate has ﬁxed interior nodes at 105 and 180 MeV, while the three-2003
knot comparator has ﬁxed nodes at 90, 160, and 220 MeV. Five-fold blocked source-only Poisson2004
deviance and a one-standard-error rule select between them. Knot locations are never ﬁtted. For2005
native 10%, the selected native-1% shape is transferred with a free normalization and only the2006
frozen, ridge-penalized, zero-mean contrast allowed by the protocol.2007
The second candidate is a three-region geometric blend in log yield. A thresholded generalized-2008
gamma low component, positive quadratic log-Chebyshev middle component, and monotone power-2009
exponential high component are joined with ﬁxed quintic tapers over 85–125 and 165–215 MeV. The2010
boundaries and tapers are ﬁxed. Native 10% again receives only the declared normalization and2011
two bounded regional contrasts; the component shapes are not reﬁtted.2012
B.7.2 Source-only model selection and qualiﬁcation2013
Five blocked source-only folds select the two-knot candidate: its mean held-out deviance per bin2014
is 1.153, compared with 1.358 for the three-knot candidate, and all candidate-fold best basins2015
repeat 22–24 times. This discrete choice does not make the selected family a qualiﬁed source mean.2016

override a failed blocked-prediction, optimizer-reproducibility, or parameter-bound gate.2018

B.7.3 Signal-inﬂuence and feature-scale audits2019
Signal rigidity is tested rather than inferred from the small number of model coordinates. The2020
frozen audit reports the projection of leave-window-out source reﬁts onto the bin-integrated sig-2021
nal template, the linearized tangent absorption, deterministic source reﬁts after one-, three-, and2022
ﬁve-sigma injections, and the separation of adjacent extrema in the ﬁtted log-mean correction. Op-2023
timizer success, constraint feasibility, and repeated recovery of the best basin are part of the audit.2024
Passing this audit does not rescue a failed source-qualiﬁcation gate.2025
The predeclared maxima are 0.20 σA for a fake-gap shift, 0.04 for the conservative tangent2026
fraction, and 0.20 for z|fabs|. Both models fail all three. In particular, the K2 basis projects as2027
much as 24.5% of a narrow template in the linearized metric, while the regional basis reaches 63.1%.2028
A small number of ﬁxed coordinates therefore does not establish that the form cannot learn signal.2029

B.7.4 Five-lane nested pseudoexperiment design2030
The ﬁve reported lanes are explicit in Table 27. Within each source family, higher exposures are2031
formed with independent Poisson increments, so the lanes are nested rather than independent.2032
Masses and injection strengths reuse each background realization. The two source families are2033
distinct, and the protocol also makes the two model streams distinct. Consequently, side-by-side2034
model diﬀerences are unpaired and do not isolate a causal response to functional-form replacement.2035
Table 27: Frozen ﬁve-lane closure inventory. Each lane contains 20 background labels per model,
ﬁve mass hypotheses, and four injection strengths, hence 400 raw extraction states per lane and
model. Repeated labels in nested lanes are not additional independent backgrounds.

B.7.5 Pull-blind length-ceiling pilot2040
Three independent pilot backgrounds per model and source family are kept disjoint from the 202041
closure backgrounds. The background-only pilot compares upper factors 15, 20, and 25 at 65,2042
120, and 210 MeV on identical inputs. The common closure ceiling is selected for all models and2043
lanes using only bound occupancy, same-input nested likelihood, optimizer reproducibility, and the2044
20-to-25 coordinate plateau. Pulls, ﬁtted amplitudes, recovery, local signiﬁcance, and limits are2045
forbidden inputs. The production v4.2 factor-15 card remains unchanged regardless of the pilot2046
outcome.2047
Factor 20 fails: seven states contact its exact/near upper bound, the maximum |∆LML |/n train2048
from 20 to 25 is 0.01535 against 0.001, and the 95th percentile of |∆ ℓ|/σ x is 5.0 against 0.03. The2049
protocol therefore uses its factor-25 fallback. Table 28 exposes a defect in that fallback rule: all2050
nine independent pilot states for the K2 native-1% lane also lie exactly at factor 25. The fallback2051
is not a demonstrated plateau.2052

B.7.6 Conditional zero-signal closure and injected recovery2067
Zero injection is reported ﬁrst. Every accepted signed pull, the raw-ﬁrst optimizer state, any2068
exclusion, the median, and the declared ﬁnite-sample mean and width intervals remain visible.2069
Nonzero results then report signed pull, pull width, change in ﬁtted signiﬁcance, and amplitude2070
recovery for the one-, three-, and ﬁve-sigma injections. The four strengths and ﬁve masses share2071
backgrounds and must not be presented as independent tests.2072
Both model collections contain exactly 2000 raw and 2000 accepted rows, 100 twenty-background2073
cells, and zero exclusions. This establishes ledger completeness, not scientiﬁc closure. Pull-blind2074
branch selection changes the ﬁrst raw attempt in 570 K2 and 444 regional states; the accepted2075
endpoints in Table 30 are therefore not replaced by raw-ﬁrst values.2076

test. The complete 200-cell ledger is archived separately.

The exploratory Holm-plus- |¯p| ≥0. 2 screen ﬂags 3 of 25 K2 zero-signal cells, all at 65 MeV, and2077
16 of 25 regional cells. The K2 median absolute zero-signal mean is 0.193 and its maximum is 1.659.2078
The regional values are 1.728 and 7.558. Regional negative oﬀsets concentrate near the 85–125 MeV2079
low/middle overlap, while large positive oﬀsets appear in the 165–215 MeV middle/high overlap.2080
This pattern is a conditional model mismatch, not evidence for a physical feature.2081
The raw ratio ˆA/A inj is strongly confounded by these zero-signal oﬀsets. Across all 1500 injected2082
rows per model, the paired response has median 0. 9804 with central 68% range 0.9678–0.9949 for2083
K2, and median 0. 9708 with range 0.9557–0.9872 for the regional blend. Cell medians are stable2084
from one to ﬁve injected reference sigmas, but rare accepted branch changes remain: K2 has 82085
responses below 0.9, 3 below 0.8, and 1 below 0.5; the regional blend has 6, 2, and 2, respectively.2086
These tails, together with the independent source-reﬁt audit, forbid a claim of signal safety.2087
B.7.7 Disposition2088
Neither residual-structured form is promoted. K2 is the less pathological conditional stress truth:2089
it has three exploratory material zero-bias ﬂags, localized at 65 MeV, compared with sixteen and2090
a strong overlap-aligned pattern for the regional blend. Nevertheless K2 fails source qualiﬁcation2091
and all signal-inﬂuence gates, has two missing native-10% constrained inﬂuence reﬁts, and leaves2092
its native-1% factor-25 closure upper-bound-censored. The separate factor-50 ceiling remediation2093
passes only for that targeted lane and does not re-evaluate the closure. The regional blend also2094
fails source qualiﬁcation and all inﬂuence gates; its zero-signal oﬀsets reach 7.558 mean-pull units2095
even though none of its extraction ﬁts contacts factor 25.2096

B.8 Asimov calibration and background-only pseudoexperiments2106
When toy strengths are speciﬁed in units of σA, the package ﬁrst constructs a reference extraction2107
uncertainty σA, ref (m) by ﬁtting the local signal-strength model to a background-only reference2108
spectrum,2109 (
ˆAref , σ A, ref
)
= F[nref ; b(m), C(m), w(m)] , (90)
where b(m) and C(m) are the GP-predicted blind-window mean and covariance, and w(m) is the2110
full-range Gaussian signal template restricted to the blinded bins. The injected strength axis is2111
then deﬁned by2112
Ainj(m) = Zinj σA, ref (m). (91)
It is useful to state explicitly what σA, ref is not. It is not the uncertainty extracted from an2113
individual toy after signal injection. Rather, it is a calibration quantity computed once from a2114
background-only reference problem at each mass hypothesis. In the code this is exactly the object2115
returned by evaluating the local ﬁt on the reference spectrum and reading oﬀ its ﬁtted σA [44, 36].2116
Its role is to deﬁne what is meant by an injected “ 1σ,” “ 2σ,” or “ 5σ” signal through Ainj = ZinjσA, ref2117
before any toy-by-toy ﬂuctuations are generated.2118
In Asimov mode , the reference spectrum is deterministic,2119
nref,i = bi, (92)
so σA, ref measures the nominal local sensitivity implied by the ﬁtted background model. In Poisson2120
mode, the reference spectrum is ﬁrst ﬂuctuated,2121
nref,i ∼ Pois(bi) , (93)
and the extracted σA, ref then inherits an extra layer of background-only sampling noise. In both2122
cases, the v3 headline closure studies use the reference spectrum only to seed a background-only2123
reﬁt. The reference uncertainty is evaluated after imposing the same mass support, exclusion2124
mask, kernel bounds, and reﬁt prescription used for the corresponding toy ensemble. This matched2125
background-only reﬁt is the calibration reference used in the updated 2021 study and in the year-2126
speciﬁc lower-bound comparisons. Earlier studies that evaluated σA, ref directly from a preﬁt Asimov2127
spectrum are retained in Appendix C; they do not deﬁne the v3 calibration.2128
This distinction matters for the later proﬁle-likelihood interpretation. The Asimov construction2129
deﬁnes the expected sensitivity scale, while the background-only pseudoexperiments are what test2130
coverage, bias, and ﬁnite-sample departures from the asymptotic approximation. Put diﬀerently:2131
the Asimov dataset calibrates the injection axis, but the pseudoexperiments remain the mecha-2132
nism that proﬁles the background model and measures how realistic background-only ﬂuctuations2133
propagate through the extraction chain.2134
The later toy summaries therefore involve two related uncertainty scales. The reference quantity2135
σA, ref ﬁxes the horizontal injection axis and the nominal target signiﬁcance Zinj. The toy-level σA2136
is returned by the ﬁt for that speciﬁc toy after the background and signal ﬂuctuations have been2137
realized. Pulls use the toy-level σA, whereas Zinj is deﬁned from σA, ref . Once the reference and2138
toy ﬁts are matched, the ensemble pull and residual-signiﬁcance calibrations should consequently2139
be mutually consistent, up to ﬁnite-sample and ﬁt ﬂuctuations. A large coherent separation is a2140
diagnostic of a reference-denominator or reﬁt mismatch; it is not accepted as normal behavior of2141
an otherwise calibrated study.2142
154

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
B.9 Poisson versus multinomial signal injection2143
Signal injection is distinct from the choice of σA reference. In Poisson-total injection , each bin2144
receives a Poisson draw with mean Ainjwi, so the realized total signal ﬂuctuates from toy to toy. In2145
multinomial ﬁxed-total injection , the total number of injected events is ﬁxed and only the bin-2146
to-bin allocation ﬂuctuates. Since the present signal template is normalized on the full histogram2147
range, Ainj denotes the total local signal yield and only the fraction fblindAinj is expected to appear2148
inside the blinded bins for the selected 2. 25 σm window.2149
This distinction matters when interpreting uncertainty bars on validation plots. Only Poisson-2150
total injection produces a genuine horizontal spread in the realized injected strength. Under ﬁxed-2151
total multinomial injection the horizontal coordinate is exact.2152
B.10 Reﬁt versus no-reﬁt extraction displays2153
The note now distinguishes two pseudoexperiment tests because they are answering diﬀerent ques-2154
tions. In the no-reﬁt display, the GP sideband model is ﬁtted once on the real data and then held2155
ﬁxed while a background realization is drawn in the blind window. This is the cleanest closure2156
test of whether the extraction ﬁt can recover an injected signal when the background prediction is2157
treated as the correct local model. In the full-reﬁt display, the toy is generated over the full mass2158
range and the GP is reﬁt on the toy sidebands before the blind-window extraction is repeated. That2159
second test is slower and more aggressive, but it is also the direct diagnostic for signal absorption2160
by the reﬁtted background model.2161
These tests should therefore not be judged by the same visual criterion. Agreement between the2162
no-reﬁt pseudoexperiment display and the observed-data display is expected and desirable because2163
both are using the same sideband-conditioned background description. The full-reﬁt display is2164
instead meant to stress the nuisance-model freedom. When it visibly degrades the extracted signal,2165
the correct interpretation is not that the no-reﬁt test was “too easy,” but that the full procedural2166
toy is probing how much the sideband reﬁt can absorb signal-like structure.2167
B.11 Representative single-mass extraction displays2168
Representative extraction displays make the statistical procedure concrete at the level of an indi-2169
vidual mass hypothesis. The 2015 30 MeV display and the 2016 10% 58 MeV display are retained2170
because they show, in real data, how the proﬁled background deformation competes with the narrow2171
signal template. The 2016 example is particularly useful because the best-ﬁt amplitude is negative.2172
Figure 127 is included to make those competing ﬁt components visible before moving to ensemble2173
summaries.2174
B.12 Pull, coverage, and signiﬁcance-calibration diagnostics2175
The central ensemble-level validation quantities are the pull,2176
pull =
ˆA − Ainj
σA
, (94)
its mean and width as a function of mass and injected strength, and the empirical coverage of the2177
nominal one- and two-standard-deviation intervals. These are standard closure diagnostics in HEP2178
statistical analyses because they test, respectively, whether the estimator is biased, whether the2179
quoted uncertainty is correctly calibrated, and whether the reported intervals achieve the claimed2180
frequentist coverage [ 44, 36].2181

(a) 2015 data at 30 MeV.
(b) 2016 10% data at 58 MeV, including a negative ˆA.
Figure 127: Representative blind-window ﬁts using data. These displays show the ﬁt components
directly and serve as concrete examples of the local extraction machinery before one moves to
ensemble diagnostics.
The main closure evidence in this section comes from the functional-form pseudoexperiments,2182
where the smooth-spectrum truth is an analytic toy seed rather than a GP-generated background2183
ensemble. Earlier GP-generated pull, coverage, and ∆ Z displays remain useful internal checks of2184

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
the extraction layer, but they are not shown as headline validation ﬁgures because the refmatched2185
functional-form pull and calibration plots are the more direct closure tests.2186
B.12.1 Representative pull distributions2187

C.5 Auxiliary shared-coupling demonstrations2255
The following plots illustrate shared-coupling bookkeeping but were generated with provisional2256
injection scenarios. They are not direct CL s coverage tests and are not used to select the v3 kernel2257
bounds. Their purpose is narrower: they show how one injected ε2 maps through the dataset-speciﬁc2258
conversion factors into diﬀerent signal yields and signiﬁcances, and how statistically independent2259
likelihoods add information about that common parameter.2260
The extraction displays below use no-reﬁt pseudoexperiments with a nominal ﬁve-standard-2261
deviation shared signal. The GP background is ﬁrst conditioned on the sidebands and then held2262
ﬁxed while the local pseudoexperiment is extracted. This isolates the signal and nuisance-parameter2263
bookkeeping of the local likelihood; it does not test whether a toy-by-toy sideband reﬁt can absorb2264
signal.2265
The ﬁnal display in this sequence answers that complementary question. Its toy was generated2266
over the full mass range and the GP was retrained on the toy sidebands before the blind-window2267
extraction. Any loss of recovery can therefore reveal signal absorption by the background reﬁt,2268
which the three no-reﬁt displays are not designed to measure.2269

HPS-ANALYSIS-NOTE BH-GPR v4.9.7
Figure 150: Historical two-dataset shared-coupling search-power scan. At each point the injected
common ε2 is translated into dataset-speciﬁc signal yields before the combined statistic is evaluated.
The curves therefore illustrate genuine accumulation of likelihood information for one coupling,
rather than a toy in which the same event count is added to every dataset. The injection scenarios
were provisional, so the plot documents the combination logic but does not quantify the v3 candidate
sensitivity.
